final isPureTideModeProvider = StateNotifierProvider<PureTideModeNotifier, bool>((ref) {
  return PureTideModeNotifier();
});

class PureTideModeNotifier extends StateNotifier<bool> {
  static const String _prefKey = 'is_pure_tide_mode_v2';
  static const String _legacyMigratedKey = 'has_migrated_pure_tide_default';

  PureTideModeNotifier() : super(false) {
    _loadPreference();
  }

  Future<void> _loadPreference() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final hasMigrated = prefs.getBool(_legacyMigratedKey) ?? false;
      final isFounder = prefs.getBool('is_founder') ?? false;

      if (isFounder && !hasMigrated) {
        state = true;
        await prefs.setBool(_prefKey, true);
        await prefs.setBool(_legacyMigratedKey, true);
      } else {
        state = prefs.getBool(_prefKey) ?? false;
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> toggle() async {
    await setMode(!state);
  }

  Future<void> setMode(bool isPure) async {
    state = isPure;
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await prefs.setBool(_prefKey, state);
    } catch (_) {}
  }
}

class HomePage extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const HomePage({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<HomePage> createState() => _HomePageState();
}

class _HomePageState extends ConsumerState<HomePage> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) async {
      if (!mounted) return;
      _checkMaritimeSafetyConsent();
      _checkLegacyVvipThemeMigration();
      FounderWelcomeDialog.checkAndShowIfNeeded(context);

      try {
        NotificationService.setNavigatorKey(appNavigatorKey);
        FcmService.setNavigatorKey(appNavigatorKey);
        await NotificationService.init();
        await FcmService.init();
      } catch (e, stack) {
        GlobalErrorTrap.recordException(
          e,
          stackTrace: stack,
          contextTag: "PushNotificationInit",
          severity: ErrorSeverity.warning,
        );
      }
    });
  }

  Future<void> _checkLegacyVvipThemeMigration() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final bool hasMigratedTheme = prefs.getBool('has_migrated_theme_v26') ?? false;
      
      if (!hasMigratedTheme) {
        final bool isPro = prefs.getBool('is_pro') ?? false;
        if (isPro) {
          ref.read(themeModeProvider.notifier).setTheme(AppThemeMode.classicLight);
        }
        await prefs.setBool('has_migrated_theme_v26', true);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _checkMaritimeSafetyConsent() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    if (!mounted) return;
    final bool hasAgreed = prefs.getBool('has_agreed_maritime_safety_v2') ?? false;
    
    if (!hasAgreed && mounted) {
      _showMandatorySafetyDisclaimer(context, prefs);
    }
  }

  void _showMandatorySafetyDisclaimer(BuildContext context, SharedPreferences prefs) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.abyssCard,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
          side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.glassBorder, width: 0.5),
        ),
        title: const Row(
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.anchor_rounded, color: AppColors.bioGold, size: 24),
            SizedBox(width: 10),
            Text(
              "老船長出海安全須知",
              style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, letterSpacing: -0.3),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        content: const Text(
          "歡迎登艦！出海作釣與航海活動具備自然不可抗力。本系統即時數據錨定氣象署官方遙測，供休閒參考，出海請務必穿著合格救生衣與防滑釘鞋，注意人身安全。",
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, color: AppColors.textSecondary, height: 1.45),
        ),
        actions: [
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            height: 48,
            child: ElevatedButton(
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: AppColors.pelagicCyan,
                foregroundColor: AppColors.abyssBlack,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                elevation: 0,
              ),
              onPressed: () async {
                HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
                await prefs.setBool('has_agreed_maritime_safety_v2', true);
                if (ctx.mounted) Navigator.pop(ctx);
              },
              child: const Text("同意並進入指揮中心", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 13.5)),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final isPureTide = ref.watch(isPureTideModeProvider);
    final tideViewAsync = ref.watch(tideViewDataProvider);
    final currentId = ref.watch(currentStationIdProvider);
    final selectedDate = ref.watch(selectedDateProvider);
    final favoritesAsync = ref.watch(favoriteStationsProvider);
    
    final bool isPro = ref.watch(premiumProvider.select((s) => s.isPremium || s.isFounder));

    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeModeProvider);
    final bool isEInk = themeMode == AppThemeMode.eInkHighContrast;
    final bool isClassicTheme = themeMode == AppThemeMode.classicLight;
    final bool isDarkAbyss = themeMode == AppThemeMode.darkAbyss;
    final bool isClassicUI = themeMode != AppThemeMode.darkAbyss;

    final allStations = ref.watch(stationListProvider).value ?? AppConstants.fallbackStations;
    final currentStation = allStations.firstWhere((s) => s.id == currentId, orElse: () => allStations.first);

    final nearestId = ref.watch(nearestStationIdProvider);
    final nearestStation = nearestId != null 
        ? allStations.firstWhere((s) => s.id == nearestId, orElse: () => allStations.first)
        : null;

    final bool isFavorited = favoritesAsync.value?.contains(currentId) ?? false;
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final String selectedKey = DateFormat('yyyyMMdd').format(selectedDate);
    final String todayKey = DateFormat('yyyyMMdd').format(now);
    final bool isToday = selectedKey == todayKey;
    final bool isFuture = selectedDate.isAfter(now) && !isToday;
    final bool isPast = !isToday && !isFuture;

    final Color modeThemeColor = isToday 
        ? const Color(0xFF1E40AF)
        : (isFuture ? const Color(0xFF2563EB) : const Color(0xFFD84315));

    final Color pageBg = isEInk ? Colors.white : (isClassicTheme ? AppColors.classicBg : AppColors.abyssBlack);
    final Color appBarBg = isEInk ? Colors.white : (isClassicTheme ? modeThemeColor : AppColors.abyssBlack.withValues(alpha: 0.88));
    final Color topIconColor = isEInk ? Colors.black : Colors.white;
    final Color ribbonThemeColor = isEInk ? Colors.black : modeThemeColor;

    final String pageTitle = isPureTide 
        ? "純潮汐航海儀表" 
        : (isToday ? "今日即時海象" : (isFuture ? "未來預報模式" : "歷史觀測回測"));

    AppThemeMode nextMode;
    IconData themeIcon;
    if (isDarkAbyss) {
      nextMode = AppThemeMode.classicLight;
      themeIcon = Icons.nights_stay_rounded;
    } else if (isClassicTheme) {
      nextMode = AppThemeMode.eInkHighContrast;
      themeIcon = Icons.wb_sunny_rounded;
    } else {
      nextMode = AppThemeMode.darkAbyss;
      themeIcon = Icons.contrast_rounded;
    }

    Widget? smartFab;
    if (!isToday) {
      smartFab = FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: () {
          HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
          ref.read(selectedDateProvider.notifier).state = DateTime.now();
        },
        backgroundColor: isEInk ? Colors.black : modeThemeColor,
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 4,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.calendar_today_rounded, size: 16),
        label: const Text("返回今日", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 13.5)),
      );
    } else if (nearestId != null && nearestId != currentId && nearestStation != null) {
      String shortName = nearestStation.name.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[\(（].*?[\)）]'), '').trim();
      if (shortName.length > 8) shortName = "${shortName.substring(0, 8)}...";
      
      smartFab = FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: () {
          HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
          ref.read(currentStationIdProvider.notifier).state = nearestId;
        },
        backgroundColor: isEInk ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFF10B981),
        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
        elevation: 6,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.my_location_rounded, size: 16),
        label: Text("切換就近測站 ($shortName)", style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 12.5)),
      );
    }

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: pageBg,
      drawer: StationDrawer(currentId: currentId),
      body: RefreshIndicator(
        color: ribbonThemeColor,
        backgroundColor: Colors.white,
        onRefresh: () async {
          HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
          try {
            return await ref.refresh(tideViewDataProvider.future);
          } catch (_) {}
        },
        child: CustomScrollView(
          physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(parent: AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics()),
          slivers: [
            SliverAppBar(
              pinned: true,
              expandedHeight: 180,
              backgroundColor: appBarBg,
              elevation: isEInk ? 1 : 0,
              leadingWidth: 84,
              leading: Builder(builder: (context) => _buildRegionButton(context, topIconColor, isEInk)),
              centerTitle: true,
              title: Text(
                pageTitle,
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: topIconColor, fontSize: 18, letterSpacing: -0.3),
              ),
              actions: [
                _buildCompactAction(
                  icon: isPureTide ? Icons.waves_rounded : Icons.radar_rounded,
                  color: isPureTide ? (isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.bioGold) : topIconColor,
                  tooltip: isPureTide ? "切換全海象" : "切換純潮汐",
                  onPressed: () {
                    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
                    ref.read(isPureTideModeProvider.notifier).toggle();
                  },
                ),
                _buildCompactAction(
                  icon: themeIcon,
                  color: topIconColor,
                  tooltip: "循環切換顯示模式",
                  onPressed: () {
                    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
                    ref.read(themeModeProvider.notifier).setTheme(nextMode);
                  },
                ),
                _buildCompactAction(
                  icon: isFavorited ? Icons.star_rounded : Icons.star_border_rounded,
                  color: isFavorited ? (isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.bioGold) : topIconColor,
                  tooltip: isFavorited ? "已收藏" : "加入收藏",
                  onPressed: () async {
                    HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
                    await ref.read(tideRepositoryProvider).toggleFavorite(currentId);
                    ref.invalidate(favoriteStationsProvider);
                  },
                ),
                _buildCompactAction(
                  icon: Icons.calendar_month_rounded,
                  color: topIconColor,
                  tooltip: "選擇日期",
                  onPressed: () {
                    HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
                    _openCalendar(context, ref);
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
              ],
              flexibleSpace: FlexibleSpaceBar(
                background: Column(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                  children: [
                    DateRibbon(selectedDate: selectedDate, themeColor: ribbonThemeColor),
                    const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            tideViewAsync.when(
              loading: () => SliverFillRemaining(
                child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: ribbonThemeColor, strokeWidth: 2.5)),
              ),
              error: (err, _) => SliverFillRemaining(
                child: _buildOfflineArmorUI(ref, currentStation.name, isClassicUI, isEInk),
              ),
              data: (viewData) {
                final station = viewData.stationData;
                final isBuoy = allStations.any((s) => s.id == currentId && s.isBuoy);

                final dayForecasts = station.forecasts.where((f) =>
                    DateFormat('yyyyMMdd').format(f.dateTime) == selectedKey).toList();

                final dayObservations = isToday
                    ? station.observations
                    : station.observations.where((o) => DateFormat('yyyyMMdd').format(o.dateTime) == selectedKey).toList();

                final Observation? activeObservation = dayObservations.isNotEmpty
                    ? dayObservations.last
                    : null;

                return SliverPadding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 120),
                  sliver: SliverList(
                    delegate: SliverChildListDelegate([
                      
                      if (isPureTide) ...[
                        _buildPaywallBlurredWrapper(
                          isPro: isPro,
                          blurMessage: "升級 PRO 解鎖經典純潮汐對照表",
                          child: ClassicPureTideTable(station: station, selectedDate: selectedDate, isClassic: isClassicUI),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      ],

                      if (isToday && !isPureTide) ...[
                        _buildPaywallBlurredWrapper(
                          isPro: isPro,
                          blurMessage: "解鎖 PRO 獲取 AI 長湧與走水安全預警",
                          child: SeaBriefingCard(station: station, distance: viewData.distanceKm),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      ],

                      StationHeader(info: station.info, distanceKm: isToday ? viewData.distanceKm : null),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),

                      if (isFuture) ...[
                        _buildSectionHeader("🌟 專家級潮汐預報 (30日)", isEInk ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFF1E40AF)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 10),

                        _buildPaywallBlurredWrapper(
                          isPro: isPro,
                          blurMessage: "解鎖 PRO 查看未來 30 天詳細滿乾潮時間",
                          child: _buildV1ForecastCard(dayForecasts.isNotEmpty ? dayForecasts : station.forecasts.take(4).toList(), isClassicUI, isEInk),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 14),

                        _buildFutureInfoCard(isClassicUI),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),

                        _buildSectionHeader("🌊 預測潮位走勢", isEInk ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFF1E40AF)),
                        const SizedBox(height: 10),
                        
                        _buildPaywallBlurredWrapper(
                          isPro: isPro,
                          blurMessage: "解鎖 PRO 查看全天候極值走勢與起流窗口",
                          child: const CustomCard(
                            child: TideChartSheet(observations: []),
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      ] 
                      else if (isPast && activeObservation == null) ...[
                        AstroHindcastCard(
                          selectedDate: selectedDate,
                          stationName: station.info.stationName,
                          isClassic: isClassicUI,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      ] 
                      else ...[
                        if (activeObservation != null) ...[
                          HeroMetricCard(current: activeObservation, isBuoy: isBuoy),
                          const SizedBox(height: 14),

                          SafetyAlert(current: activeObservation),
                          const SizedBox(height: 14),

                          CustomCard(
                            child: TideChartSheet(observations: dayObservations),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 20),

                          if (!isPureTide) ...[
                            _buildSectionHeader("📋 詳細觀測參數", isEInk ? Colors.black : modeThemeColor),
                            const SizedBox(height: 12),
                            MetricGrid(current: activeObservation),
                            const SizedBox(height: 16),

                            WindCompassCard(current: activeObservation),
                            const SizedBox(height: 20),
                          ],
                        ],
                      ],

                      if (!isPureTide) ...[
                        SolunarCard(selectedDate: selectedDate),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      ],

                      if (!isPureTide) ...[
                        BiteRadarSection(
                          station: station,
                          selectedDate: selectedDate,
                          isClassic: isClassicUI,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      ],

                      if (isToday && !isPureTide) ...[
                        UgcRadarCard(
                          stationId: currentId,
                          stationName: station.info.stationName,
                          waveHeight: activeObservation?.waveHeight,
                          windSpeed: activeObservation?.windSpeed,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      ],

                      if (!isPureTide) ...[
                        _buildSectionHeader("🏪 港區補給與海事支援", isEInk ? Colors.black : modeThemeColor),
                        const SizedBox(height: 12),
                        LocalMerchantCard(
                          stationName: station.info.stationName,
                          region: currentStation.region,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      ],

                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      _buildV1Footer(isFuture, isClassicUI),
                    ]),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),

      floatingActionButton: smartFab,
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPaywallBlurredWrapper({
    required bool isPro,
    required String blurMessage,
    required Widget child,
  }) {
    if (isPro) return child;

    return Stack(
      children: [
        ClipRRect(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
          child: ImageFiltered(
            imageFilter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 8.0, sigmaY: 8.0),
            child: Opacity(
              opacity: 0.6,
              child: IgnorePointer(child: child),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Positioned.fill(
          child: Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
            ),
            child: Center(
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.lock_rounded, color: AppColors.bioGold, size: 32),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    Text(
                      blurMessage,
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.white,
                        fontSize: 13.5,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                        shadows: [Shadow(color: Colors.black54, blurRadius: 4)],
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 14),
                    SizedBox(
                      width: double.infinity,
                      height: 44,
                      child: ElevatedButton(
                        style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                          backgroundColor: AppColors.bioGold,
                          foregroundColor: Colors.black87,
                          elevation: 8,
                          shadowColor: AppColors.bioGold.withValues(alpha: 0.5),
                          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                        ),
                        onPressed: () {
                          HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
                          Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const PremiumPage()));
                        },
                        child: const Text("立即解鎖", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 14)),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildV1ForecastCard(List<TideForecast> forecasts, bool isClassicUI, bool isEInk) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isClassicUI ? Colors.white : AppColors.abyssCard,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
        border: Border.all(color: isClassicUI ? (isEInk ? Colors.black : Colors.grey.shade100) : AppColors.glassBorder, width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 0.5),
        boxShadow: (isClassicUI && !isEInk) ? [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.03), blurRadius: 10, offset: const Offset(0, 4))] : null,
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
      child: Column(
        children: forecasts.map((f) {
          final bool isHigh = f.tideType.contains("滿");
          return Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10.0),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      isHigh ? Icons.arrow_upward_rounded : Icons.arrow_downward_rounded,
                      color: isEInk ? Colors.black : (isHigh ? const Color(0xFFEF4444) : const Color(0xFF3B82F6)),
                      size: 22,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 14),
                    Text(
                      "${DateFormat('HH:mm').format(f.dateTime)} - ${f.tideType}",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 15,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                        color: isClassicUI ? const Color(0xFF0F172A) : AppColors.textPrimary,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                Text(
                  "${f.tideHeight} cm",
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 17,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                    color: isEInk ? Colors.black : (isClassicUI ? const Color(0xFF334155) : AppColors.pelagicCyan),
                    fontFeatures: const [FontFeature.tabularFigures()],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        }).toList(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFutureInfoCard(bool isClassicUI) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isClassicUI ? Colors.white : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.04),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: isClassicUI ? Colors.grey.shade100 : AppColors.glassBorder, width: 0.5),
      ),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Icon(Icons.memory_rounded, color: isClassicUI ? const Color(0xFF3B82F6) : AppColors.pelagicCyan, size: 22),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  "M4 物理調和與天體推演引擎",
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 13.5,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                    color: isClassicUI ? const Color(0xFF0F172A) : AppColors.textPrimary,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  "未來時空預測模式已啟動。系統正以天體引力模型結合氣象署歷史參數進行高維度推算。實況波高與陣風數據將於該日由實體感測器回傳。",
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 12,
                    color: isClassicUI ? Colors.grey.shade600 : AppColors.textSecondary,
                    height: 1.45,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildV1Footer(bool isFuture, bool isClassicUI) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(
          "SSOT 官方數據庫節點",
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 13,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
            color: isClassicUI ? const Color(0xFF0F172A) : AppColors.textPrimary,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(
          isFuture
              ? "天體引力模型推算中 (非即時感測)"
              : "中央氣象署官方 30 天數據備份節點。光纖數據每小時更新以確保單一真相來源。",
          style: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 11.5,
            color: isClassicUI ? Colors.grey.shade500 : AppColors.textTertiary,
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 30),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSectionHeader(String title, Color accentColor) {
    return Text(
      title,
      style: TextStyle(
        fontSize: 15,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
        color: accentColor,
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRegionButton(BuildContext context, Color topIconColor, bool isEInk) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 14.0, top: 12, bottom: 12),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: () {
          HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
          Scaffold.of(context).openDrawer();
        },
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        child: Container(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: isEInk ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05) : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.18),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            border: Border.all(color: isEInk ? Colors.black26 : Colors.white30, width: 0.5),
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.map_outlined, color: topIconColor, size: 14),
              const SizedBox(width: 4),
              Text(
                "地區", 
                style: TextStyle(color: topIconColor, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCompactAction({
    required IconData icon,
    required Color color,
    required String tooltip,
    required VoidCallback? onPressed,
  }) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: 36,
      height: 36,
      child: IconButton(
        padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
        icon: Icon(icon, color: color, size: 20),
        tooltip: tooltip,
        onPressed: onPressed,
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildOfflineArmorUI(WidgetRef ref, String stationName, bool isClassicUI, bool isEInk) {
    final Color textColor = isEInk ? Colors.black : (isClassicUI ? AppColors.classicText : AppColors.textPrimary);
    final Color iconColor = isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.bioGold;

    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 28),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.offline_bolt_rounded, size: 48, color: iconColor),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
              "[$stationName] 天文潮位接管中", 
              style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 16, color: textColor),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            ElevatedButton(
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                backgroundColor: isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.bioGold,
                foregroundColor: isEInk ? Colors.white : AppColors.abyssBlack,
              ),
              onPressed: () => ref.refresh(tideViewDataProvider),
              child: const Text("重新整理", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _openCalendar(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final DateTime? picked = await showDatePicker(
      context: context,
      initialDate: ref.read(selectedDateProvider),
      firstDate: now.subtract(const Duration(days: 30)),
      lastDate: now.add(const Duration(days: 30)),
    );
    if (picked != null && mounted) {
      ref.read(selectedDateProvider.notifier).state = picked;
    }
  }
}

class AstroHindcastCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final DateTime selectedDate;
  final String stationName;
  final bool isClassic;

  const AstroHindcastCard({
    super.key,
    required this.selectedDate,
    required this.stationName,
    required this.isClassic,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final solunar = SolunarUtil.calculate(selectedDate);
    final dateStr = DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd').format(selectedDate);

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isClassic ? Colors.white : AppColors.abyssCard,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(
          color: isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade200 : AppColors.glassBorder,
          width: 0.5,
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.history_toggle_off_rounded, color: AppColors.bioGold, size: 20),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text(
                "歷史天文潮位推演 [$stationName]",
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                  fontSize: 14,
                  color: isClassic ? AppColors.classicText : AppColors.textPrimary,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            "觀測日 $dateStr 歷史實測資料已歸檔，系統已為您啟動天文引力模型推演：當日適逢${solunar.tideCategory}（${solunar.lunarDateStr}），潮汐係數 ${solunar.tidalCoefficient}，魚群活躍指數 ${solunar.fishActivityScore}%。",
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              color: isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade600 : AppColors.textSecondary,
              height: 1.45,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class BiteRadarSection extends StatelessWidget {
  final TideStationData station;
  final DateTime selectedDate;
  final bool isClassic;

  const BiteRadarSection({
    super.key,
    required this.station,
    required this.selectedDate,
    required this.isClassic,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final biteResult = BitePredictionEngine.predict(
      stationData: station,
      targetDate: selectedDate,
    );

    final Color primaryColor = isClassic ? const Color(0xFF0077B6) : AppColors.pelagicCyan;
    final Color textColor = isClassic ? AppColors.classicText : AppColors.textPrimary;

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isClassic ? Colors.white : AppColors.abyssCard,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(
          color: isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade200 : AppColors.glassBorder,
          width: 0.5,
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.radar_rounded, color: primaryColor, size: 20),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Text(
                    "指標魚種索餌活性雷達",
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                      fontSize: 14,
                      color: textColor,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 3),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  biteResult.biteLevel,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 11,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                    color: primaryColor,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Text(
            "黃金起流窗口：${biteResult.primaryWindow}",
            style: const TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: AppColors.bioGold,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          Text(
            biteResult.oceanTriggerSummary,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 11.5,
              color: isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade600 : AppColors.textSecondary,
              height: 1.4,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          ...biteResult.speciesIndices.map((sp) {
            return Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4.0),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    sp.speciesName,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 12.5,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      color: textColor,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        "${sp.statusBadge} (${sp.biteProbability}%)",
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 12,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                          color: sp.biteProbability >= 75 ? const Color(0xFF30D158) : primaryColor,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          }),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


=== lib/features/tide/presentation/station_guide_page.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../providers/tide_provider.dart';
import '../../../core/utils/constants.dart';
import '../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../../shared/widgets/custom_card.dart';
import '../../premium/services/premium_service.dart';
import '../../premium/presentation/premium_page.dart';

/// 🌟 Apple 首席設計工藝：全台 85 測站水文百科指南
/// 100% 商業防線閉環與三模適配版：
/// 1. 徹底封死免費用戶透過百科指南繞過付費牆偷渡 VIP 測站的商業漏洞！
/// 2. 77 席 VIP 測站強制掛載金色 PRO 鎖頭標章，點擊自動精確引流至付費牆。
/// 3. 深度適配 E-Ink 烈日抗反光黑白主題，消除強光下的泥濘色彩。
class StationGuidePage extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const StationGuidePage({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<StationGuidePage> createState() => _StationGuidePageState();
}

class _StationGuidePageState extends ConsumerState<StationGuidePage> {
  String _selectedRegion = "全部";
  String _searchQuery = "";

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeModeProvider);
    final bool isEInk = themeMode == AppThemeMode.eInkHighContrast;
    final bool isClassic = themeMode == AppThemeMode.classicLight;

    final stationsAsync = ref.watch(stationListProvider);
    final premiumState = ref.watch(premiumProvider);
    final bool isPro = premiumState.isPremium || premiumState.isFounder;

    final Color bgColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.white 
        : (isClassic ? AppColors.classicBg : AppColors.abyssBlack);
    final Color appBarBg = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? const Color(0xFF0077B6) : AppColors.abyssBlack.withValues(alpha: 0.88));
    final Color accentColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? const Color(0xFF0077B6) : AppColors.pelagicCyan);

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: bgColor,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text(
          "85 測站水文百科指南", 
          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 17, color: Colors.white, letterSpacing: -0.4),
        ),
        backgroundColor: appBarBg,
        iconTheme: const IconThemeData(color: Colors.white),
        elevation: isClassic ? 1 : 0,
      ),
      body: CustomScrollView(
        physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(parent: AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics()),
        slivers: [
          SliverToBoxAdapter(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 8),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  _buildEducationalCard(isClassic, isEInk),
                  const SizedBox(height: 18),
                  _buildSearchBar(isClassic, isEInk, accentColor),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  _buildRegionFilter(isClassic, isEInk, accentColor),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          stationsAsync.when(
            loading: () => SliverFillRemaining(
              child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: accentColor, strokeWidth: 2.5)),
            ),
            error: (err, _) => SliverFillRemaining(
              child: Center(
                child: Text("測站載入異常: $err", style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.hazardCoral, fontSize: 13)),
              ),
            ),
            data: (allStations) {
              final filtered = allStations.where((s) {
                final matchRegion = _selectedRegion == "全部" || s.region == _selectedRegion;
                final matchQuery = _searchQuery.isEmpty ||
                    s.name.contains(_searchQuery) ||
                    s.id.toLowerCase().contains(_searchQuery.toLowerCase());
                return matchRegion && matchQuery;
              }).toList();

              if (filtered.isEmpty) {
                return SliverFillRemaining(
                  child: Center(
                    child: Text(
                      "未找到符合條件的水文測站", 
                      style: TextStyle(color: isEInk ? Colors.black54 : (isClassic ? Colors.grey : AppColors.textTertiary), fontSize: 13),
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              }

              return SliverPadding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 8, 16, 40),
                sliver: SliverList(
                  delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
                    (context, index) => _buildStationItem(filtered[index], isPro, isClassic, isEInk, accentColor),
                    childCount: filtered.length,
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildEducationalCard(bool isClassic, bool isEInk) {
    final Color iconAccent = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? const Color(0xFF0077B6) : AppColors.pelagicCyan);
    final Color titleColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? const Color(0xFF023E8A) : AppColors.textPrimary);

    return CustomCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(7),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: isEInk ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.08) : iconAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: Icon(Icons.menu_book_rounded, color: iconAccent, size: 16),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Text(
                "老船長作戰速查指南", 
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 14, color: titleColor, letterSpacing: -0.2),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          _buildKnowledgeRow("🌊 湧浪週期 > 8 秒", "外海具深層長湧，岸邊極易突發瘋狗浪，礁石務必穿戴合格釘鞋救生衣。", isClassic, isEInk),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          _buildKnowledgeRow("🌡️ 水溫變動 > 1.5℃", "冷暖水塊交匯走水急促，黑毛、鱸魚活性提高但索餌就餌轉為敏感。", isClassic, isEInk),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          _buildKnowledgeRow("⏱️ 滿乾潮前後 2 分水", "潮差走水帶動浮游藻類與餌魚聚集，為全天作釣最佳「黃金爆咬窗口」。", isClassic, isEInk),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildKnowledgeRow(String title, String desc, bool isClassic, bool isEInk) {
    final Color titleColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? const Color(0xFFD97706) : AppColors.bioGold);
    final Color descColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black87 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.blueGrey.shade800 : AppColors.textSecondary);

    return Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(title, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: titleColor)),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        Expanded(
          child: Text(
            desc, 
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, color: descColor, height: 1.4),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSearchBar(bool isClassic, bool isEInk, Color accentColor) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isEInk ? Colors.white : (isClassic ? Colors.white : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.06)),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(
          color: isEInk ? Colors.black : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade300 : AppColors.glassBorder), 
          width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 0.5,
        ),
        boxShadow: (isClassic && !isEInk) ? [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.02), blurRadius: 4)] : null,
      ),
      child: TextField(
        onChanged: (val) => setState(() => _searchQuery = val),
        style: TextStyle(color: isEInk ? Colors.black : (isClassic ? Colors.black87 : AppColors.textPrimary), fontSize: 13.5),
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          hintText: "搜尋測站地名或代號 (如: 富貴角 / C6AH2)...",
          hintStyle: TextStyle(color: isEInk ? Colors.black38 : (isClassic ? Colors.grey : AppColors.textTertiary), fontSize: 12.5),
          prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search_rounded, size: 18, color: isEInk ? Colors.black : accentColor),
          border: InputBorder.none,
          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildRegionFilter(bool isClassic, bool isEInk, Color accentColor) {
    final regions = ["全部", ...AppConstants.regions];
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      child: Row(
        children: regions.map((r) {
          final isSelected = _selectedRegion == r;

          Color chipBg;
          Color chipText;
          Border? chipBorder;

          if (isEInk) {
            chipBg = isSelected ? Colors.black : Colors.white;
            chipText = isSelected ? Colors.white : Colors.black;
            chipBorder = Border.all(color: Colors.black, width: isSelected ? 1.5 : 1.0);
          } else if (isClassic) {
            chipBg = isSelected ? const Color(0xFF0077B6) : Colors.white;
            chipText = isSelected ? Colors.white : Colors.blueGrey.shade700;
            chipBorder = isSelected ? null : Border.all(color: Colors.grey.shade300, width: 0.5);
          } else {
            chipBg = isSelected ? AppColors.textPrimary : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.06);
            chipText = isSelected ? AppColors.abyssBlack : AppColors.textSecondary;
            chipBorder = isSelected ? null : Border.all(color: AppColors.glassBorder, width: 0.5);
          }

          return Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8.0),
            child: InkWell(
              onTap: () {
                HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
                setState(() => _selectedRegion = r);
              },
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
              child: AnimatedContainer(
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 6.5),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: chipBg,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  border: chipBorder,
                  boxShadow: (isSelected && !isEInk) 
                      ? [BoxShadow(color: accentColor.withValues(alpha: 0.3), blurRadius: 8)] 
                      : null,
                ),
                child: Text(
                  r,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: chipText,
                    fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.w900 : FontWeight.w600,
                    fontSize: 12,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        }).toList(),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStationItem(
    StationModel station, 
    bool isProUser, 
    bool isClassic, 
    bool isEInk,
    Color accentColor,
  ) {
    final bool isLocked = station.isProOnly && !isProUser;

    final Color itemBg = isEInk 
        ? Colors.white 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.white : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.04));
    final Color borderColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade200 : AppColors.glassBorder);
    final Color titleColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? AppColors.classicText : AppColors.textPrimary);
    final Color subtitleColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black54 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade600 : AppColors.textTertiary);
    final Color typeColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (station.isBuoy ? Colors.indigoAccent : accentColor);

    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: itemBg,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: borderColor, width: isEInk ? 1.5 : (isClassic ? 0.8 : 0.5)),
        boxShadow: (isClassic && !isEInk) ? [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.02), blurRadius: 6, offset: const Offset(0, 2))] : null,
      ),
      child: Material(
        color: Colors.transparent,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        child: ListTile(
          onTap: () {
            HapticFeedback.lightImpact();

            if (isLocked) {
              Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const PremiumPage()));
              return;
            }

            ref.read(currentStationIdProvider.notifier).state = station.id;
            ref.read(selectedDateProvider.notifier).state = DateTime.now();
            Navigator.pop(context);
          },
          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 4),
          leading: Container(
            width: 36,
            height: 36,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: isEInk ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.08) : typeColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
              border: Border.all(color: isEInk ? Colors.black : typeColor.withValues(alpha: 0.3), width: isEInk ? 1.0 : 0.5),
            ),
            child: Icon(
              isLocked 
                  ? Icons.lock_rounded 
                  : (station.isBuoy ? Icons.sensors_rounded : Icons.water_drop_rounded), 
              color: isLocked ? (isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.bioGold) : typeColor, 
              size: 16,
            ),
          ),
          title: Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  station.name,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, 
                    fontSize: 13.5, 
                    color: isLocked ? (isEInk ? Colors.black45 : Colors.grey) : titleColor,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),

              if (isLocked)
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 7, vertical: 2),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.bioGold.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                    border: Border.all(color: isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.bioGold.withValues(alpha: 0.4), width: 0.5),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.workspace_premium_rounded, size: 10, color: isEInk ? Colors.white : AppColors.bioGold),
                      const SizedBox(width: 2),
                      Text(
                        "PRO",
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 9.5, 
                          color: isEInk ? Colors.white : AppColors.bioGold, 
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                )
              else
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 7, vertical: 2),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: isEInk ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.08) : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade100 : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.08)),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                    border: Border.all(color: borderColor, width: 0.5),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    station.stationType,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 9.5, 
                      color: typeColor, 
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
          subtitle: Text(
            "${station.agency} (${station.id}) · ${station.region}海域", 
            style: TextStyle(color: subtitleColor, fontSize: 10.5, height: 1.3),
          ),
          trailing: Icon(
            isLocked ? Icons.arrow_forward_ios_rounded : Icons.check_circle_outline_rounded, 
            size: 14, 
            color: isLocked ? (isEInk ? Colors.black45 : AppColors.bioGold) : subtitleColor,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


=== lib/features/tide/presentation/tide_chart_sheet.dart ===

import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import '../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../data/tide_model.dart';

/// 🌟 經海事光學對比重塑之潮位與波高趨勢圖表 (Tide Chart Sheet)
/// 100% 三模光學適配版：徹底消滅深色模式下深藍曲線隱沒在黑卡片的視覺盲區！
class TideChartSheet extends ConsumerWidget {
  final List<Observation> observations;

  const TideChartSheet({
    super.key,
    required this.observations,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeModeProvider);
    final bool isEInk = themeMode == AppThemeMode.eInkHighContrast;
    final bool isClassic = themeMode == AppThemeMode.classicLight;
    final bool isDark = themeMode == AppThemeMode.darkAbyss;

    // 🌟 核心修復：語意化動態色彩矩陣，消滅深色背景吃線
    final Color lineColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? const Color(0xFF0077B6) : AppColors.pelagicCyan);
    final Color titleColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.blueGrey.shade800 : AppColors.textPrimary);
    final Color axisColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black54 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade600 : AppColors.textSecondary);
    final Color gridColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black12 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.withValues(alpha: 0.1) : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.05));
    final Color dateChipBg = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black12 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.blueGrey.withValues(alpha: 0.1) : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.06));
    final Color dateChipText = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.blueGrey.shade800 : AppColors.textSecondary);
    final Color tooltipBg = isEInk 
        ? Colors.white 
        : (isClassic ? const Color(0xFF023E8A) : AppColors.abyssSurface);

    if (observations.isEmpty) {
      return SizedBox(
        height: 200,
        child: Center(
          child: Text(
            "目前無圖表觀測數據", 
            style: TextStyle(color: axisColor, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    final String chartDate = DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd').format(observations.first.dateTime);

    final bool useWaveHeight = observations.any((o) => o.tideHeight == null) &&
                               observations.any((o) => o.waveHeight != null);

    return Column(
      children: [
        // --- 圖表標題與日期 ---
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Row(
                children: [
                  Container(width: 12, height: 3.5, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: lineColor, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2))),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Text(
                    useWaveHeight ? "實測波高趨勢 (m)" : "即時潮位趨勢 (m)",
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: titleColor),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 2.5),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: dateChipBg,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                  border: isEInk ? Border.all(color: Colors.black, width: 1.0) : null,
                ),
                child: Text(
                  chartDate,
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: dateChipText, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        
        // --- 核心圖表區 ---
        Container(
          height: 220,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 20, top: 12),
          child: LineChart(
            LineChartData(
              gridData: FlGridData(
                show: true,
                drawVerticalLine: false,
                horizontalInterval: 1,
                getDrawingHorizontalLine: (value) => FlLine(
                  color: gridColor,
                  strokeWidth: isEInk ? 1.0 : 0.8,
                ),
              ),
              titlesData: FlTitlesData(
                show: true,
                rightTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
                topTitles: const AxisTitles(sideTitles: SideTitles(showTitles: false)),
                
                // 下方 X 軸時間
                bottomTitles: AxisTitles(
                  sideTitles: SideTitles(
                    showTitles: true,
                    reservedSize: 30,
                    interval: 4, 
                    getTitlesWidget: (value, meta) {
                      final int index = value.toInt();
                      if (index >= 0 && index < observations.length) {
                        final String timeStr = DateFormat('HH:mm').format(observations[index].dateTime);
                        return SideTitleWidget(
                          axisSide: meta.axisSide,
                          space: 8,
                          child: Text(timeStr, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: axisColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500)),
                        );
                      }
                      return const SizedBox.shrink();
                    },
                  ),
                ),
                
                // 左側 Y 軸高度
                leftTitles: AxisTitles(
                  sideTitles: SideTitles(
                    showTitles: true,
                    getTitlesWidget: (value, meta) {
                      return Text(
                        "${value.toStringAsFixed(1)}m",
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: axisColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                      );
                    },
                    reservedSize: 36,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              borderData: FlBorderData(show: false),
              
              lineBarsData: [
                LineChartBarData(
                  spots: observations.asMap().entries.map((e) {
                    final double yValue = useWaveHeight 
                        ? (e.value.waveHeight ?? 0.0) 
                        : (e.value.tideHeight ?? 0.0);
                    return FlSpot(e.key.toDouble(), yValue);
                  }).toList(),
                  isCurved: true, 
                  curveSmoothness: 0.35, 
                  color: lineColor,
                  barWidth: isEInk ? 3.5 : 3.0,
                  isStrokeCapRound: true,
                  dotData: const FlDotData(show: false), 
                  belowBarData: BarAreaData(
                    show: !isEInk, // 🌟 E-Ink 模式徹底關閉漸層，杜絕泥濘髒灰
                    gradient: LinearGradient(
                      begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                      end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                      colors: [
                        lineColor.withValues(alpha: isDark ? 0.35 : 0.25),
                        lineColor.withValues(alpha: 0.0),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
              
              lineTouchData: LineTouchData(
                touchTooltipData: LineTouchTooltipData(
                  tooltipBgColor: tooltipBg,
                  tooltipRoundedRadius: 10,
                  tooltipBorder: isEInk ? const BorderSide(color: Colors.black, width: 1.5) : const BorderSide(color: AppColors.glassBorder, width: 0.5),
                  getTooltipItems: (List<LineBarSpot> touchedBarSpots) {
                    return touchedBarSpots.map((barSpot) {
                      final obs = observations[barSpot.x.toInt()];
                      final String fullTime = DateFormat('MM/dd HH:mm').format(obs.dateTime);
                      return LineTooltipItem(
                        "$fullTime\n",
                        TextStyle(color: isEInk ? Colors.black : Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 11),
                        children: [
                          TextSpan(
                            text: "${barSpot.y.toStringAsFixed(2)} m",
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.pelagicCyan, 
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, 
                              fontSize: 14.5,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      );
                    }).toList();
                  },
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}


=== lib/features/tide/presentation/widgets/classic_pure_tide_table.dart ===

﻿import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

import '../../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../../../core/utils/solunar_util.dart';
import '../../data/tide_model.dart';
import '../../../../shared/widgets/custom_card.dart';

/// 🌟 100% 依據 V1 原始碼 DNA 還原之老船長經典純潮汐對照表
/// 100% 時態保真與物理單位修復版：
/// 1. 徹底消滅歷史無資料日時，偷抓未來預報來充數的「時空幻覺」重大詐欺漏洞！
/// 2. 實裝智能單位格式化器 (_formatHeight)，消滅 LINE 戰報出現 1.85cm 的尷尬瑕疵。
class ClassicPureTideTable extends StatelessWidget {
  final TideStationData station;
  final DateTime selectedDate;
  final bool isClassic;

  const ClassicPureTideTable({
    super.key,
    required this.station,
    required this.selectedDate,
    required this.isClassic,
  });

  /// 🌟 智慧物理單位格式化器 (防呆)
  String _formatHeight(String h) {
    final v = double.tryParse(h);
    if (v == null) return "$h cm";
    // 若含有小數點或小於 10，代表底層已歸一化為公尺 (m)
    if (v.abs() < 10.0 || h.contains('.')) return "${v.toStringAsFixed(2)} m";
    // 否則維持公分 (cm)
    return "${v.toStringAsFixed(0)} cm";
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final solunar = SolunarUtil.calculate(selectedDate);
    final selectedKey = DateFormat('yyyyMMdd').format(selectedDate);
    
    // 依選定日期過濾預報節點
    final dayForecasts = station.forecasts.where((f) =>
        DateFormat('yyyyMMdd').format(f.dateTime) == selectedKey).toList();
    
    // 🚨 核心防禦：絕不拿未來的預報 (take(4)) 來填補歷史的空白！維持 SSOT 單一真相！
    final sortedForecasts = List<TideForecast>.from(dayForecasts)
      ..sort((a, b) => a.dateTime.compareTo(b.dateTime));

    final Color textColor = isClassic ? AppColors.classicText : AppColors.textPrimary;
    final Color subTextColor = isClassic ? Colors.blueGrey.shade700 : AppColors.textSecondary;
    final Color goldColor = isClassic ? const Color(0xFFD97706) : AppColors.bioGold;

    return CustomCard(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 12),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          // 1. 頂部經典標題列與一鍵分享至 LINE 快捷鍵
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 4),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(Icons.anchor_rounded, color: goldColor, size: 18),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Text(
                      "老船長經典潮汐表",
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 15,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                        color: textColor,
                        letterSpacing: -0.3,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 6),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5, vertical: 1.5),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: goldColor.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                        border: Border.all(color: goldColor.withValues(alpha: 0.35), width: 0.5),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        "V1 經典排印",
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: goldColor),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                IconButton(
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.copy_rounded, size: 16),
                  tooltip: "複製純文字至 LINE 群組",
                  color: subTextColor,
                  onPressed: () => _copyTideTableToClipboard(context, solunar, sortedForecasts),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          
          // 2. 簡練天體水象綱要
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(6, 0, 6, 6),
            child: Text(
              "${solunar.lunarDateStr} • ${solunar.moonPhaseName} • ${solunar.tideCategory} (係數 ${solunar.tidalCoefficient})",
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: goldColor),
            ),
          ),

          Divider(height: 1, color: isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade200 : AppColors.glassBorder),

          // 3. 原汁原味 V1 原始碼 DNA 實體排版
          if (sortedForecasts.isEmpty)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 24),
              child: Center(
                child: Text(
                  "該日無潮汐預報資料或已歸檔\n請參考上方天文物理調和模型走勢",
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: TextStyle(color: subTextColor, fontSize: 12.5, height: 1.5),
                ),
              ),
            )
          else
            ...sortedForecasts.map((f) {
              final bool isHigh = f.tideType.contains("滿");
              return ListTile(
                dense: true,
                contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 0),
                leading: Icon(
                  isHigh ? Icons.arrow_upward : Icons.arrow_downward, 
                  color: isHigh ? Colors.redAccent : Colors.blueAccent, 
                  size: 20,
                ),
                title: Text(
                  "${DateFormat('HH:mm').format(f.dateTime)} - ${f.tideType}",
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    fontSize: 14.5,
                    color: textColor,
                  ),
                ),
                trailing: Text(
                  _formatHeight(f.tideHeight),
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                    fontSize: 16.5,
                    color: isClassic ? Colors.blueGrey.shade800 : AppColors.pelagicCyan,
                    fontFeatures: const [FontFeature.tabularFigures()],
                  ),
                ),
              );
            }),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _copyTideTableToClipboard(BuildContext context, SolunarData solunar, List<TideForecast> list) {
    HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
    final stationName = station.info.stationName;
    final dateStr = DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd').format(selectedDate);

    final StringBuffer sb = StringBuffer();
    sb.writeln("🌊【老船長經典潮汐表】$stationName");
    sb.writeln("📅 日期：$dateStr (${solunar.lunarDateStr}) | ${solunar.tideCategory}");
    sb.writeln("-----------------------------");
    if (list.isEmpty) {
      sb.writeln("⚠️ 該日預報已歸檔，以實測為主");
    } else {
      for (final f in list) {
        final t = DateFormat('HH:mm').format(f.dateTime);
        sb.writeln("$t - ${f.tideType.padRight(4)} ${_formatHeight(f.tideHeight)}");
      }
    }
    sb.writeln("-----------------------------");
    sb.writeln("📲 潮汐表 Pro 85 測站光纖直連：https://beigou0427.github.io/tide_forecast_app/");

    Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: sb.toString()));
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(
        content: Text("✅ 已複製純文字潮汐表，可直接貼至 LINE 群組！"),
        duration: Duration(seconds: 3),
        behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
      ),
    );
  }
}

=== lib/features/tide/presentation/widgets/date_ribbon.dart ===

﻿import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

import '../../providers/tide_provider.dart';
import '../../../premium/services/premium_service.dart';
import '../../../premium/presentation/premium_page.dart';

/// 🌟 Dan Abramov 細粒度響應式日期捲軸 (Fine-Grained Reactive Ribbon)
/// 100% 幾何狀態同步版：
/// 1. 實裝 didUpdateWidget，確保透過日曆或「返回今日」按鈕變更日期時，捲軸能自動精確滾動至目標可視區。
/// 2. 導入 ref.watch(select()) 精確訂閱與冪等點擊防抖，徹底阻止無效全域重繪。
class DateRibbon extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  final DateTime selectedDate;
  final Color themeColor;
  const DateRibbon({super.key, required this.selectedDate, required this.themeColor});

  @override
  ConsumerState<DateRibbon> createState() => _DateRibbonState();
}

class _DateRibbonState extends ConsumerState<DateRibbon> {
  late ScrollController _scrollController;
  static const double itemWidth = 66.0;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _scrollController = ScrollController();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _scrollToSelectedDate(animate: false));
  }

  // 🌟 核心防線：監聽外部 State 變化，強制對齊 ScrollController 幾何偏移量！
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant DateRibbon oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.selectedDate != widget.selectedDate) {
      // 若外部變更了日期 (例如日曆選擇或返回今日)，自動平滑滾動至該日期
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _scrollToSelectedDate(animate: true));
    }
  }

  void _scrollToSelectedDate({required bool animate}) {
    if (_scrollController.hasClients) {
      final now = DateTime.now();
      final todayZero = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
      final targetZero = DateTime(widget.selectedDate.year, widget.selectedDate.month, widget.selectedDate.day);
      
      // 精算日曆天數差，並約束在 [-30, 30] 快取區間內
      final int dayDiff = targetZero.difference(todayZero).inDays.clamp(-30, 30);
      final int targetIndex = 30 + dayDiff; // Index 30 為今日
      
      final screenWidth = MediaQuery.of(context).size.width;
      final offset = (targetIndex * itemWidth) - (screenWidth / 2) + (itemWidth / 2);
      
      if (animate) {
        _scrollController.animateTo(
          offset, 
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400), 
          curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
        );
      } else {
        _scrollController.jumpTo(offset);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _scrollController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final DateTime now = DateTime.now();
    final DateTime todayZero = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
    final String todayStr = DateFormat('yyyyMMdd').format(todayZero);
    final String selectedStr = DateFormat('yyyyMMdd').format(widget.selectedDate);

    // 🌟 Dan Abramov 細粒度選擇器：僅在 isPremium 布林值翻轉時才觸發局部重繪
    final bool isPremium = ref.watch(premiumProvider.select((s) => s.isPremium || s.isFounder));

    return SizedBox(
      height: 78,
      child: ListView.builder(
        controller: _scrollController,
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10),
        itemCount: 61, 
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final int dayDiff = index - 30;
          final date = DateTime(todayZero.year, todayZero.month, todayZero.day + dayDiff);
          
          final String currentStr = DateFormat('yyyyMMdd').format(date);
          final bool isToday = currentStr == todayStr;
          final bool isSelected = currentStr == selectedStr;
          final bool isWeekend = date.weekday == DateTime.saturday || date.weekday == DateTime.sunday;

          final bool isFree = dayDiff >= -1 && dayDiff <= 1;
          final bool isLocked = !isFree && !isPremium;

          final Color weekdayColor = isSelected 
              ? widget.themeColor 
              : (isToday ? Colors.amberAccent : (isWeekend ? Colors.deepOrangeAccent : Colors.white70));
          
          final Color dateColor = isSelected 
              ? widget.themeColor 
              : (isWeekend && !isToday ? Colors.orange.shade100 : Colors.white);

          return GestureDetector(
            onTap: () {
              // 🌟 冪等性短路防護：若點擊當前已選中日期，0 操作直接返回
              if (isSelected) return;

              HapticFeedback.selectionClick();

              if (isLocked) {
                Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const PremiumPage()));
              } else {
                ref.read(selectedDateProvider.notifier).state = date;
              }
            },
            child: AnimatedContainer(
              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
              width: 58,
              margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4, vertical: 8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isSelected 
                    ? Colors.white 
                    : (isWeekend && !isToday ? Colors.deepOrange.withValues(alpha: 0.15) : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.15)),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                border: isToday 
                    ? Border.all(color: isSelected ? Colors.amber : Colors.amberAccent, width: 2.5) 
                    : (isWeekend && !isSelected ? Border.all(color: Colors.deepOrangeAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.4), width: 1.2) : null),
                boxShadow: isSelected ? [const BoxShadow(color: Colors.black26, blurRadius: 4)] : null,
              ),
              child: Stack(
                children: [
                  Center(
                    child: Column(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          DateFormat('E').format(date), 
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: weekdayColor, 
                            fontSize: 10, 
                            fontWeight: isToday || isWeekend ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
                          ),
                        ),
                        Text(
                          DateFormat('dd').format(date), 
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: dateColor, 
                            fontSize: 18, 
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (isToday)
                    Positioned(
                      top: -11, left: 0, right: 0, 
                      child: Center(
                        child: Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2), 
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.amber, 
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), 
                            boxShadow: const [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black12, blurRadius: 2)],
                          ), 
                          child: const Text("今日", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontSize: 8, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  if (isLocked)
                    Positioned(
                      top: 4, right: 4, 
                      child: Icon(Icons.lock_outline, size: 10, color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.7)),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

=== lib/features/tide/presentation/widgets/developer_master_panel.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
