                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text(
                        "突發湧浪困礁、落水意外或海難事故，請立即撥打",
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: isEInk ? Colors.black54 : (isClassic ? Colors.red.shade700 : Colors.white70)),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                OutlinedButton(
                  style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                    side: BorderSide(color: emergencyColor, width: 1.0),
                    foregroundColor: emergencyColor,
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  ),
                  onPressed: () => _showEmergencyCallDialog(context, isClassic, isEInk),
                  child: const Text("撥打 118", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 11)),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showEmergencyCallDialog(BuildContext context, bool isClassic, bool isEInk) {
    HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: isEInk ? Colors.white : (isClassic ? Colors.white : AppColors.abyssCard),
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        title: const Row(
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.warning_amber_rounded, color: AppColors.hazardCoral, size: 24),
            SizedBox(width: 8),
            Text("海巡署緊急救援通報", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 16)),
          ],
        ),
        content: const Text(
          "即將撥打海洋委員會海巡署【118】免付費緊急救援服務專線。\n\n如遇人員落海、礁石受困或緊急海難，請保持冷靜向值班人員報出當前測站地名與地標方位。",
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 13, height: 1.5),
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
            child: const Text("取消"),
          ),
          ElevatedButton(
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
              backgroundColor: AppColors.hazardCoral,
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
            ),
            onPressed: () async {
              Navigator.pop(ctx);
              final Uri url = Uri.parse("tel:118");
              if (await canLaunchUrl(url)) {
                await launchUrl(url);
              }
            },
            child: const Text("確認撥打 118", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _launchMapSearch(BuildContext context, String query) async {
    final Uri googleMapUrl = Uri.https('www.google.com', '/maps/search/', {'api': '1', 'query': query});
    try {
      if (await canLaunchUrl(googleMapUrl)) {
        await launchUrl(googleMapUrl, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
      } else {
        final Uri appleMapUrl = Uri.https('maps.apple.com', '/', {'q': query});
        if (await canLaunchUrl(appleMapUrl)) {
          await launchUrl(appleMapUrl, mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (context.mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(content: Text("地圖開啟異常: $e"), behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating),
        );
      }
    }
  }
}


=== lib/features/tide/presentation/widgets/metric_grid.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:google_fonts/google_fonts.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../data/tide_model.dart';

/// 🌟 經海事光學對比重塑之 6 大水文參數矩陣
/// 100% 三模配色自適應版：徹底消滅深色模式下白底白字的視覺災難！
class MetricGrid extends ConsumerWidget {
  final Observation current;

  const MetricGrid({super.key, required this.current});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeModeProvider);
    final bool isEInk = themeMode == AppThemeMode.eInkHighContrast;
    final bool isClassic = themeMode == AppThemeMode.classicLight;
    final bool isDark = themeMode == AppThemeMode.darkAbyss;

    // 🌟 核心修復：語意化動態色彩矩陣，消滅寫死白色卡片與隱形文字
    final Color tileBg = isEInk 
        ? Colors.white 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.white : AppColors.abyssCard);
    final Color tileBorder = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade200 : AppColors.glassBorder);
    final Color labelColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black54 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade600 : AppColors.textSecondary);
    final Color valueColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? AppColors.classicText : AppColors.textPrimary);

    final List<Widget> metrics = [];

    void addIfValid(String label, double? val, String unit, IconData icon, Color iconColor) {
      if (val != null) {
        metrics.add(_buildMetricTile(
          label, 
          "$val$unit", 
          icon, 
          isEInk ? Colors.black : iconColor,
          tileBg,
          tileBorder,
          labelColor,
          valueColor,
          isEInk,
        ));
      }
    }

    addIfValid("波浪高度", current.waveHeight, " m", Icons.waves_rounded, Colors.indigoAccent);
    addIfValid("海水溫度", current.seaTemperature, " ℃", Icons.thermostat_rounded, const Color(0xFFFF9500));
    addIfValid("觀測風速", current.windSpeed, " m/s", Icons.air_rounded, const Color(0xFF10B981));
    addIfValid("海流流速", current.currentSpeed, " m/s", Icons.explore_rounded, Colors.tealAccent);
    addIfValid("風向角度", current.windDirection, "°", Icons.navigation_rounded, Colors.brown);
    addIfValid("大氣壓力", current.airPressure, " hPa", Icons.speed_rounded, Colors.purpleAccent);
    addIfValid("即時氣溫", current.airTemperature, " ℃", Icons.wb_sunny_rounded, AppColors.bioGold);

    if (metrics.isEmpty) {
      return Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Center(
          child: Text(
            "該測站目前僅提供基礎潮位資訊", 
            style: TextStyle(color: labelColor, fontSize: 13),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    return GridView.count(
      shrinkWrap: true,
      physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
      crossAxisCount: 2,
      crossAxisSpacing: 12,
      mainAxisSpacing: 12,
      childAspectRatio: 1.7,
      children: metrics,
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMetricTile(
    String label, 
    String value, 
    IconData icon, 
    Color iconColor,
    Color tileBg,
    Color tileBorder,
    Color labelColor,
    Color valueColor,
    bool isEInk,
  ) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: tileBg,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: tileBorder, width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 0.5),
        boxShadow: (isEInk || tileBg != Colors.white) ? null : [
          BoxShadow(color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.03), blurRadius: 8, offset: const Offset(0, 2))
        ],
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: isEInk ? Colors.black12 : iconColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
              shape: BoxShape.circle,
            ),
            child: Icon(icon, color: iconColor, size: 18),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Text(label, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: labelColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500)),
                const SizedBox(height: 2),
                FittedBox(
                  fit: BoxFit.scaleDown,
                  child: Text(
                    value, 
                    style: GoogleFonts.rubik(
                      fontSize: 16.5, 
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, 
                      color: valueColor,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          )
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


=== lib/features/tide/presentation/widgets/safety_alert.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../data/tide_model.dart';

/// 🌟 經海事夜航暗適應規範重塑之安全警示條 (Safety Alert)
/// 100% 三模光學適配版：消滅深色夜間模式下寫死粉紅/淺綠的刺眼眩光！
class SafetyAlert extends ConsumerWidget {
  final Observation current;

  const SafetyAlert({super.key, required this.current});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeModeProvider);
    final bool isEInk = themeMode == AppThemeMode.eInkHighContrast;
    final bool isClassic = themeMode == AppThemeMode.classicLight;
    final bool isDark = themeMode == AppThemeMode.darkAbyss;

    // 浪高 > 1.5m 或 風速 > 8m/s 視為海象不佳
    final bool isDanger = (current.waveHeight ?? 0) > 1.5 || (current.windSpeed ?? 0) > 8.0;

    // 🌟 核心修復：語意化動態夜視色彩矩陣，消滅刺眼粉彩
    final Color alertBg;
    final Color alertBorder;
    final Color iconColor;
    final Color textColor;

    if (isEInk) {
      alertBg = Colors.white;
      alertBorder = Colors.black;
      iconColor = Colors.black;
      textColor = Colors.black;
    } else if (isClassic) {
      alertBg = isDanger ? Colors.red.shade50 : Colors.green.shade50;
      alertBorder = isDanger ? Colors.red.shade200 : Colors.green.shade200;
      iconColor = isDanger ? Colors.red.shade700 : Colors.green.shade700;
      textColor = isDanger ? Colors.red.shade900 : Colors.green.shade900;
    } else {
      // 深淵黑金 (暗夜外礁模式)：微光半透明背景，保護瞳孔暗適應
      alertBg = isDanger 
          ? AppColors.hazardCoral.withValues(alpha: 0.12)
          : const Color(0xFF10B981).withValues(alpha: 0.10);
      alertBorder = isDanger 
          ? AppColors.hazardCoral.withValues(alpha: 0.45)
          : const Color(0xFF10B981).withValues(alpha: 0.35);
      iconColor = isDanger ? AppColors.hazardCoral : const Color(0xFF34D399);
      textColor = isDanger ? const Color(0xFFFF8595) : const Color(0xFF6EE7B7);
    }

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: alertBg,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(
          color: alertBorder,
          width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 0.8,
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Icon(
            isDanger ? Icons.warning_amber_rounded : Icons.check_circle_outline_rounded,
            color: iconColor,
            size: 22,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              isDanger 
                  ? "海況警告：現場浪高或陣風已達作業上限，不建議從事岸邊作釣！" 
                  : "海況平穩：目前環境適合作業，出海請穿著合格救生衣與釘鞋。",
              style: TextStyle(
                color: textColor,
                fontSize: 12.5,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                height: 1.4,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


=== lib/features/tide/presentation/widgets/sea_briefing_card.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../../../../core/services/tts_service.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../../../core/utils/share_util.dart';
import '../../data/tide_model.dart';

enum OperationalTier { halt, advisory, operational }

/// 🌟 經海事流體力學與 TQM 單一真相來源重塑之決策推論矩陣
class MaritimeDecision {
  final OperationalTier tier;
  final String badgeText;
  final String title;
  final String actionAdvice;
  final IconData icon;

  const MaritimeDecision({
    required this.tier,
    required this.badgeText,
    required this.title,
    required this.actionAdvice,
    required this.icon,
  });

  Color getDecisionColor() {
    switch (tier) {
      case OperationalTier.halt:
        return AppColors.hazardCoral;
      case OperationalTier.advisory:
        return const Color(0xFFFF9500);
      case OperationalTier.operational:
        return const Color(0xFF10B981);
    }
  }

  factory MaritimeDecision.evaluate({
    required int safetyScore,
    required double waveHeight,
    required double wavePeriod,
    required double windSpeed,
    double waveEnergyFlux = 0.0,
    double? airPressure,
  }) {
    // 1. 致命長湧瘋狗浪 (波能通量臨界值或深層長週期)
    final bool isDeepSwellRogue = (wavePeriod >= 10.0 && waveHeight >= 0.7) || waveEnergyFlux >= 3.5;
    
    // 2. 氣壓驟降鋒面暴潮共振 (低壓 + 長湧相長干涉)
    final bool isBarometricSwellCombo = airPressure != null && airPressure < 1005.0 && wavePeriod >= 8.5;

    // 3. 巨浪強風作業死線
    final bool isExtremeSea = waveHeight >= 2.2 || windSpeed >= 11.0;

    final bool isHalt = safetyScore < 40 || 
                        isExtremeSea || 
                        isDeepSwellRogue || 
                        isBarometricSwellCombo;

    final bool isAdvisory = !isHalt && 
                           (safetyScore < 70 || waveHeight > 1.2 || windSpeed > 7.0 || (airPressure != null && airPressure < 1010.0));

    if (isHalt) {
      String haltTitle = "極端危險 · 嚴禁外礁登礁";
      String haltAdvice = "外海偵測到致命長週期長湧或巨浪！具極端蓋礁瘋狗浪風險，嚴禁無防護水上作業！";
      
      // 🌟 核心修復：airPressure 已自動晉升為非空，直接使用 . 呼叫
      if (isBarometricSwellCombo) {
        haltTitle = "低壓暴潮警示 · 嚴禁登礁";
        haltAdvice = "大氣氣壓降至 ${airPressure.toStringAsFixed(1)}hPa 疊加外海長湧，近岸洗岸巨浪動能劇烈，請即刻撤離！";
      } else if (isDeepSwellRogue) {
        haltTitle = "長湧瘋狗浪警戒 · 嚴禁登礁";
        haltAdvice = "外海深層長湧波能達 ${waveEnergyFlux.toStringAsFixed(1)} kW/m！即便岸際風平浪靜亦具突發蓋礁危險，禁止登礁！";
      }

      return MaritimeDecision(
        tier: OperationalTier.halt,
        badgeText: "HALT 警戒",
        title: haltTitle,
        actionAdvice: haltAdvice,
        icon: Icons.dangerous_rounded,
      );
    } else if (isAdvisory) {
      return const MaritimeDecision(
        tier: OperationalTier.advisory,
        badgeText: "ADVISORY 戒備",
        title: "水文戒備 · 需穿著合格裝備",
        actionAdvice: "潮位變換急促或風浪稍強，建議避開迎風迎浪面，僅限具備背風條件之安全標點。",
        icon: Icons.warning_amber_rounded,
      );
    } else {
      return const MaritimeDecision(
        tier: OperationalTier.operational,
        badgeText: "OPERATIONAL 許可",
        title: "常態水文許可 · 仍須安全戒備",
        actionAdvice: "實測波高與風力處於常態作業水準。自然水域變幻莫測，請全程著合格救生衣與釘鞋。",
        icon: Icons.check_circle_rounded,
      );
    }
  }
}

/// 🌟 經海事流體力學與客觀感測器陣列重塑之海象簡報卡
class SeaBriefingCard extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  final TideStationData station;
  final double? distance;

  const SeaBriefingCard({super.key, required this.station, this.distance});

  @override
  ConsumerState<SeaBriefingCard> createState() => _SeaBriefingCardState();
}

class _SeaBriefingCardState extends ConsumerState<SeaBriefingCard> {
  final GlobalKey _cardBoundaryKey = GlobalKey();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeModeProvider);
    final bool isEInk = themeMode == AppThemeMode.eInkHighContrast;

    final ai = widget.station.aiBriefing;
    final obs = widget.station.observations.isNotEmpty ? widget.station.observations.last : null;
    final isSpeaking = ref.watch(ttsProvider);

    final double realWaveH = obs?.waveHeight ?? 0.0;
    final double realWaveP = obs?.wavePeriod ?? 0.0;
    final double realWindS = obs?.windSpeed ?? 0.0;
    final double flux = obs?.waveEnergyFlux ?? (realWaveH > 0 && realWaveP > 0 ? (0.49 * realWaveH * realWaveH * realWaveP) : 0.0);
    final String tideStatus = obs?.tideLevel ?? "平穩";

    final decision = MaritimeDecision.evaluate(
      safetyScore: ai?.safetyScore ?? 80,
      waveHeight: realWaveH,
      wavePeriod: realWaveP,
      windSpeed: realWindS,
      waveEnergyFlux: flux,
      airPressure: obs?.airPressure,
    );
    
    final String stationDisplayName = widget.station.info.stationName.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[\(（].*?[\)）]'), '').trim();

    final bool hasWaveSensor = obs?.waveHeight != null;
    final bool hasTideSensor = obs?.tideHeight != null;

    final String rightMetricLabel;
    final String rightMetricValue;
    final IconData rightMetricIcon;

    if (hasWaveSensor) {
      rightMetricLabel = "即時波高";
      rightMetricValue = "${obs!.waveHeight!.toStringAsFixed(1)} m";
      rightMetricIcon = Icons.surfing_rounded;
    } else if (hasTideSensor) {
      rightMetricLabel = "即時潮位";
      rightMetricValue = "${obs!.tideHeight!.toStringAsFixed(2)} m";
      rightMetricIcon = Icons.water_drop_rounded;
    } else {
      rightMetricLabel = "實測水文";
      rightMetricValue = "--";
      rightMetricIcon = Icons.sensors_off_rounded;
    }

    final BoxDecoration cardDecoration;
    final Color textColor;
    final Color subTextColor;
    final Color dividerColor;

    if (isEInk) {
      cardDecoration = BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        border: Border.all(color: Colors.black, width: 2.0),
      );
      textColor = Colors.black;
      subTextColor = Colors.black87;
      dividerColor = Colors.black26;
    } else {
      cardDecoration = BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          colors: decision.tier == OperationalTier.halt
              ? const [Color(0xFF6B1115), Color(0xFF38070A)]
              : const [Color(0xFF0052A3), Color(0xFF003870)],
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: (decision.tier == OperationalTier.halt ? const Color(0xFF6B1115) : const Color(0xFF003870)).withValues(alpha: 0.35),
            blurRadius: 16,
            offset: const Offset(0, 6),
          ),
        ],
      );
      textColor = Colors.white;
      subTextColor = Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.75);
      dividerColor = Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.2);
    }

    return RepaintBoundary(
      key: _cardBoundaryKey,
      child: Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        decoration: cardDecoration,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      Text("📍", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: textColor)),
                      const SizedBox(width: 6),
                      Flexible(
                        child: Text(
                          widget.distance != null 
                              ? "距離最近的觀測站 (${widget.distance!.toStringAsFixed(1)}km)"
                              : "距離最近的觀測站",
                          maxLines: 1,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: subTextColor,
                            fontSize: 12.5,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    InkWell(
                      onTap: () {
                        HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
                        ShareUtil.captureAndShare(
                          _cardBoundaryKey,
                          stationName: widget.station.info.stationName,
                        );
                      },
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      child: Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: isEInk ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.08) : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
                        ),
                        child: Icon(
                          Icons.ios_share_rounded,
                          color: isEInk ? Colors.black : Colors.white,
                          size: 16,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),

                    InkWell(
                      onTap: () {
                        HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
                        ref.read(ttsProvider.notifier).toggleBriefing(widget.station);
                      },
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      child: Container(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: isEInk ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.08) : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
                        ),
                        child: Icon(
                          isSpeaking ? Icons.graphic_eq_rounded : Icons.volume_up_rounded,
                          color: isEInk ? Colors.black : Colors.white,
                          size: 16,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),

            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    stationDisplayName,
                    maxLines: 1,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 26,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                      color: textColor,
                      letterSpacing: -0.5,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 9, vertical: 4),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: isEInk 
                        ? Colors.black 
                        : decision.getDecisionColor().withValues(alpha: 0.25),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                    border: Border.all(
                      color: isEInk ? Colors.black : decision.getDecisionColor(),
                      width: 1.0,
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Icon(
                        decision.icon, 
                        size: 13, 
                        color: isEInk ? Colors.white : decision.getDecisionColor(),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 4),
                      Text(
                        decision.badgeText,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontSize: 10,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                          letterSpacing: 0.5,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),

            Divider(
              height: 1, 
              thickness: 0.8, 
              color: dividerColor,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.waves_rounded, color: subTextColor, size: 22),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      Text(
                        "目前潮況",
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          color: subTextColor,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        tideStatus,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 17,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                          color: textColor,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),

                Container(
                  height: 42,
                  width: 0.8,
                  color: dividerColor,
                ),

                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      Icon(rightMetricIcon, color: subTextColor, size: 22),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      Text(
                        rightMetricLabel,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 11.5,
                          color: subTextColor,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        rightMetricValue,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 17,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                          color: textColor,
                          letterSpacing: -0.2,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),

            Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isEInk 
                    ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05) 
                    : Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.22),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                border: Border.all(
                  color: isEInk ? Colors.black12 : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
                  width: 0.5,
                ),
              ),
              child: Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Icon(
                    decision.tier == OperationalTier.halt ? Icons.warning_rounded : Icons.anchor_rounded, 
                    color: decision.tier == OperationalTier.halt ? AppColors.hazardCoral : const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                    size: 20,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            Text(
                              "老船長快報",
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: decision.tier == OperationalTier.halt ? AppColors.hazardCoral : const Color(0xFFFBBF24),
                                fontSize: 13,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                                letterSpacing: 0.2,
                              ),
                            ),
                            if (decision.tier == OperationalTier.halt) ...[
                              const SizedBox(width: 6),
                              Container(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5, vertical: 1),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: AppColors.hazardCoral.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                                ),
                                child: Text(
                                  decision.title,
                                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.hazardCoral, fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ],
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 4),
                        Text(
                          decision.tier == OperationalTier.halt 
                              ? decision.actionAdvice 
                              : (ai?.briefing ?? decision.actionAdvice),
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: isEInk ? Colors.black87 : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.9),
                            fontSize: 12,
                            height: 1.45,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


=== lib/features/tide/presentation/widgets/shareable_report_card.dart ===

﻿import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import '../../data/tide_model.dart';

class ShareableReportCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final GlobalKey boundaryKey;
  final TideStationData station;

  const ShareableReportCard({
    super.key,
    required this.boundaryKey,
    required this.station,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final ai = station.aiBriefing;
    final obs = station.observations.isNotEmpty ? station.observations.last : null;
    final nowStr = DateFormat('yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm').format(DateTime.now());

    return RepaintBoundary(
      key: boundaryKey,
      child: Container(
        width: 360,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: const Color(0xFF021B33),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF00B4D8).withValues(alpha: 0.4), width: 1.5),
        ),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: const Color(0xFF00B4D8).withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                        shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      ),
                      child: const Icon(Icons.anchor_rounded, color: Color(0xFF00B4D8), size: 18),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    const Text(
                      "TIDE PRO 老船長海象情報",
                      style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 13, letterSpacing: 1),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                Text(
                  nowStr,
                  style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.4), fontSize: 10),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      station.info.stationName,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                    ),
                    Text(
                      station.info.attr,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF00B4D8), fontSize: 12),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: (ai?.safetyScore ?? 80) >= 70 ? Colors.green.withValues(alpha: 0.2) : Colors.red.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    border: Border.all(
                      color: (ai?.safetyScore ?? 80) >= 70 ? Colors.greenAccent : Colors.redAccent,
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        "${ai?.safetyScore ?? 80}",
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
                      ),
                      Text("安全指針", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.6), fontSize: 9)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.06),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              ),
              child: Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  const Icon(Icons.auto_awesome, color: Colors.amberAccent, size: 18),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      ai?.briefing ?? "海象平穩，適合近岸作業與作釣。",
                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 13, height: 1.4),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            if (obs != null)
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround,
                children: [
                  _buildMetricItem("浪高", "${obs.waveHeight ?? '--'} m"),
                  _buildMetricItem("風速", "${obs.windSpeed ?? '--'} m/s"),
                  _buildMetricItem("潮高", "${obs.tideHeight ?? '--'} m"),
                  _buildMetricItem("水溫", "${obs.seaTemperature ?? '--'} ℃"),
                ],
              ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Divider(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.1), height: 1),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    const Text("出海作釣、潛水必備決策工具", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white60, fontSize: 10)),
                    Text("App Store 搜尋: Tide Pro", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.35), fontSize: 9)),
                  ],
                ),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.amberAccent,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                  ),
                  child: const Text("官方即時數據", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.black87, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildMetricItem(String label, String value) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(label, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.5), fontSize: 11)),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(value, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
      ],
    );
  }
}



=== lib/features/tide/presentation/widgets/solunar_card.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../../../core/utils/solunar_util.dart';
import '../../../../shared/widgets/custom_card.dart';

/// 🌟 經海事光學對比重塑之月相農曆與咬度指數儀表 (Solunar Card)
/// 100% 三模光學適配版：消滅深色模式下進度條亮白軌道與低對比建議框！
class SolunarCard extends ConsumerWidget {
  final DateTime selectedDate;
  const SolunarCard({super.key, required this.selectedDate});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeModeProvider);
    final bool isEInk = themeMode == AppThemeMode.eInkHighContrast;
    final bool isClassic = themeMode == AppThemeMode.classicLight;
    final bool isDark = themeMode == AppThemeMode.darkAbyss;

    final solunar = SolunarUtil.calculate(selectedDate);
    final bool isSpringTide = solunar.tideCategory == "大潮";

    // 🌟 核心修復：語意化動態色彩矩陣，消滅刺眼白色進度軌道
    final Color textPrimary = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? AppColors.classicText : AppColors.textPrimary);
    final Color textSecondary = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black54 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade600 : AppColors.textSecondary);
    final Color accentColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? const Color(0xFF0077B6) : AppColors.pelagicCyan);
    final Color springTideColor = isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.hazardCoral;
    final Color progressTrackColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black12 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade200 : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.08));
    final Color adviceBoxBg = isEInk 
        ? Colors.white 
        : (isClassic ? const Color(0xFF0077B6).withValues(alpha: 0.05) : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.04));
    final Color adviceBorderColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade200 : AppColors.glassBorder);

    return CustomCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Row(
                children: [
                  Text(solunar.moonPhaseEmoji, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 26)),
                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                  Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            solunar.moonPhaseName,
                            style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 14.5, color: textPrimary),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          Text(
                            "(${solunar.lunarDateStr})",
                            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: accentColor, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text("日月引力與海流活躍指數", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: textSecondary)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: isEInk ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.08) : (isSpringTide
                      ? springTideColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12)
                      : accentColor.withValues(alpha: 0.12)),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  border: Border.all(
                    color: isEInk ? Colors.black : (isSpringTide
                        ? springTideColor.withValues(alpha: 0.4)
                        : accentColor.withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
                    width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 0.8,
                  ),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      isSpringTide ? Icons.local_fire_department_rounded : Icons.water_rounded,
                      color: isSpringTide ? springTideColor : accentColor,
                      size: 14,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Text(
                      solunar.tideCategory,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: isSpringTide ? springTideColor : accentColor,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                        fontSize: 12,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          Row(
            children: [
              Text("咬度指數", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: textSecondary)),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Expanded(
                child: ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                  child: LinearProgressIndicator(
                    value: solunar.fishActivityScore / 100.0,
                    backgroundColor: progressTrackColor,
                    valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(
                      isEInk ? Colors.black : (solunar.fishActivityScore >= 85 ? AppColors.bioGold : accentColor),
                    ),
                    minHeight: 6,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              Text(
                "${solunar.fishActivityScore}%",
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontSize: 12.5,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                  color: isEInk ? Colors.black : (solunar.fishActivityScore >= 85 ? AppColors.bioGold : accentColor),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: adviceBoxBg,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              border: Border.all(color: adviceBorderColor, width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 0.5),
            ),
            child: Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.tips_and_updates_outlined, size: 15, color: accentColor),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    solunar.biteWindowAdvice,
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11.5, color: textSecondary, height: 1.4, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}


=== lib/features/tide/presentation/widgets/station_drawer.dart ===

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:google_fonts/google_fonts.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';

import '../../../../core/utils/constants.dart';
import '../../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../../providers/tide_provider.dart';
import '../../../premium/services/premium_service.dart';
import '../../../premium/presentation/premium_page.dart';
import '../../../premium/presentation/vip_center_page.dart';
import '../../../diagnostic/presentation/diagnostic_page.dart';
import '../../../onboarding/presentation/onboarding_page.dart';
import '../station_guide_page.dart';
import '../../../catch_log/presentation/catch_log_page.dart';

class StationDrawer extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  final String currentId;
  const StationDrawer({super.key, required this.currentId});

  @override
  ConsumerState<StationDrawer> createState() => _StationDrawerState();
}

class _StationDrawerState extends ConsumerState<StationDrawer> {
  String _searchQuery = "";
  String _selectedFilter = "全部";

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final themeMode = ref.watch(themeModeProvider);
    final bool isEInk = themeMode == AppThemeMode.eInkHighContrast;
    final bool isClassic = themeMode == AppThemeMode.classicLight;

    final Color drawerBg = isEInk 
        ? Colors.white 
        : (isClassic ? AppColors.classicBg : AppColors.abyssSurface);
    final Color cardBg = isEInk 
        ? Colors.white 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.white : AppColors.abyssCard);
    final Color cardBorder = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade200 : AppColors.glassBorder);
    final Color textPrimary = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black 
        : (isClassic ? AppColors.classicText : AppColors.textPrimary);
    final Color textSecondary = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black54 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade600 : AppColors.textSecondary);
    final Color searchBg = isEInk 
        ? Colors.white 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.white : Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.06));
    final Color dividerColor = isEInk 
        ? Colors.black26 
        : (isClassic ? Colors.grey.shade200 : AppColors.glassBorder);
    final Color goldColor = isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.bioGold;

    final premiumState = ref.watch(premiumProvider);
    final favoriteIdsAsync = ref.watch(favoriteStationsProvider);
    final stationListAsync = ref.watch(stationListProvider);
    final bool isPro = premiumState.isPremium || premiumState.isFounder;

    return Drawer(
      backgroundColor: drawerBg,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          _buildDrawerHeader(premiumState.isFounder, isClassic, isEInk),
          _buildPremiumEntry(premiumState, cardBg, cardBorder, textPrimary, textSecondary, isClassic, isEInk),

          // 🌟 免費用戶專屬：抽屜直通「填問卷免綁卡換 7 天 PRO」轉換橫幅
          if (!isPro)
            _buildDrawerSurveyTrialBanner(context, isEInk),

          _buildSearchField(searchBg, cardBorder, textPrimary, textSecondary, isClassic, isEInk),
          _buildFilterChips(cardBg, cardBorder, textPrimary, textSecondary, isClassic, isEInk),

          Expanded(
            child: stationListAsync.when(
              loading: () => Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.pelagicCyan)),
              error: (err, stack) => Center(child: Text("清單載入失敗", style: TextStyle(color: textSecondary))),
              data: (allStations) {
                return ListView(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  children: [
                    favoriteIdsAsync.when(
                      data: (favIds) {
                        if (favIds.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
                        final favStations = allStations.where((s) => favIds.contains(s.id)).toList();
                        if (favStations.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
                        return Theme(
                          data: Theme.of(context).copyWith(dividerColor: Colors.transparent),
                          child: ExpansionTile(
                            initiallyExpanded: true,
                            iconColor: goldColor,
                            collapsedIconColor: goldColor,
                            leading: Icon(Icons.stars, color: goldColor, size: 20),
                            title: Text("我的最愛", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: goldColor)),
                            children: favStations.map((s) => _buildStationTile(s, true, isPro, textPrimary, textSecondary, isClassic, isEInk)).toList(),
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                      loading: () => const SizedBox.shrink(),
                      error: (_, __) => const SizedBox.shrink(),
                    ),

                    Divider(height: 1, color: dividerColor),

                    ...AppConstants.regions.map((region) {
                      final List<StationModel> stations = allStations.where((s) {
                        final bool matchesRegion = s.region == region;
                        final bool matchesSearch = s.name.contains(_searchQuery) || s.id.contains(_searchQuery);
                        
                        bool matchesFilter = true;
                        if (_selectedFilter == "👑 VIP專屬") {
                          matchesFilter = s.isProOnly;
                        } else if (_selectedFilter == "🆓 免費體驗") {
                          matchesFilter = !s.isProOnly;
                        } else if (_selectedFilter == "🌊 資料浮標") {
                          matchesFilter = s.isBuoy;
                        } else if (_selectedFilter == "⏱️ 潮位站") {
                          matchesFilter = !s.isBuoy;
                        }

                        return matchesRegion && matchesSearch && matchesFilter;
                      }).toList();

                      if (stations.isEmpty && (_searchQuery.isNotEmpty || _selectedFilter != "全部")) {
                        return const SizedBox.shrink();
                      }

                      return Theme(
                        data: Theme.of(context).copyWith(dividerColor: Colors.transparent),
                        child: ExpansionTile(
                          initiallyExpanded: _searchQuery.isNotEmpty || _selectedFilter != "全部",
                          iconColor: textPrimary,
                          collapsedIconColor: textSecondary,
                          leading: CircleAvatar(
                            radius: 14,
                            backgroundColor: isEInk ? Colors.black12 : const Color(0xFF0077B6).withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                            child: Text(region[0], style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: isEInk ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFF0077B6), fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                          ),
                          title: Row(
                            children: [
                              Text(region, style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16, color: textPrimary)),
                              const SizedBox(width: 8),
                              Text("(${stations.length})", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: textSecondary)),
                            ],
                          ),
                          children: stations.map((s) {
                            final isFav = favoriteIdsAsync.value?.contains(s.id) ?? false;
                            return _buildStationTile(s, isFav, isPro, textPrimary, textSecondary, isClassic, isEInk);
                          }).toList(),
                        ),
                      );
                    }),
                  ],
                );
              },
            ),
          ),

          Divider(height: 1, color: dividerColor),
          
          // 🌟 核心重塑：剔除假特約！換成真正實用的「海事安全與救難支援 (118)」
          ListTile(
            dense: true,
            leading: const Icon(Icons.emergency_rounded, color: AppColors.hazardCoral),
            title: Row(
              children: [
                Text("海事安全與救難專線", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: textPrimary)),
                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5, vertical: 1),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: AppColors.hazardCoral.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                  ),
                  child: const Text(
                    "海巡 118", 
                    style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.hazardCoral, fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            trailing: Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 16, color: textSecondary),
            onTap: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              _showMaritimeSupportDialog(context, cardBg, textPrimary, textSecondary, cardBorder, isClassic, isEInk);
            },
          ),

          ListTile(
            dense: true,
            leading: const Icon(Icons.phishing_rounded, color: Colors.indigoAccent),
            title: Text("潮汐漁獲日誌", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: textPrimary)),
            trailing: Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 16, color: textSecondary),
            onTap: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const CatchLogPage()));
            },
          ),
          ListTile(
            dense: true,
            leading: const Icon(Icons.menu_book_rounded, color: Color(0xFF00B4D8)),
            title: Text("85 測站水文指引", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: textPrimary)),
            trailing: Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 16, color: textSecondary),
            onTap: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const StationGuidePage()));
            },
          ),
          ListTile(
            dense: true,
            leading: const Icon(Icons.health_and_safety_outlined, color: Colors.tealAccent),
            title: Text("系統自檢中心", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: textPrimary)),
            trailing: Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 16, color: textSecondary),
            onTap: () {
              Navigator.pop(context);
              Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const DiagnosticPage()));
            },
          ),
          Divider(height: 1, color: dividerColor),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8, bottom: 24),
            child: Text("資料來源：中央氣象署 (CWA)", style: GoogleFonts.notoSansTc(fontSize: 10, color: textSecondary)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDrawerSurveyTrialBanner(BuildContext context, bool isEInk) {
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 0, 12, 8),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: () {
          HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
          Navigator.pop(context);
          Navigator.push(
            context,
            MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const OnboardingPage(initialCohort: 'survey_trial')),
          );
        },
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 8),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            gradient: isEInk ? null : const LinearGradient(
              colors: [Color(0xFF0F3B2A), Color(0xFF051C12)],
              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
              end: Alignment.bottomRight,
            ),
            color: isEInk ? Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05) : null,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            border: Border.all(color: isEInk ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFF10B981), width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 1.0),
          ),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.card_giftcard_rounded, color: Color(0xFF10B981), size: 16),
              const SizedBox(width: 8),
              const Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  "填 4 題問卷，免綁卡直接領 7 天 PRO 試用！",
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: Color(0xFF34D399)),
                ),
              ),
              Icon(Icons.arrow_forward_ios_rounded, size: 12, color: isEInk ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFF34D399)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDrawerHeader(bool isFounder, bool isClassic, bool isEInk) {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 50, 24, 20),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: isEInk ? Colors.black : null,
        gradient: isEInk ? null : const LinearGradient(
          colors: [Color(0xFF023E8A), Color(0xFF0077B6)],
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              const Icon(Icons.analytics_rounded, color: Colors.white, size: 36),
              if (isFounder)
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    gradient: const LinearGradient(colors: [Color(0xFFFFD700), Color(0xFFFFA000)]),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                    boxShadow: const [BoxShadow(color: Colors.black26, blurRadius: 4)],
                  ),
                  child: const Row(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.workspace_premium, color: Colors.black87, size: 14),
                      SizedBox(width: 4),
                      Text("創始席位", style: TextStyle(color: Colors.black87, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Text(
            "海象監測指揮中心",
            style: GoogleFonts.notoSansTc(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 22, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPremiumEntry(
    PremiumState state, 
    Color cardBg, 
    Color cardBorder, 
    Color textPrimary, 
    Color textSecondary,
    bool isClassic,
    bool isEInk,
  ) {
    if (state.isFounder || state.isPremium) {
      return InkWell(
        onTap: () {
          Navigator.pop(context);
          Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const VipCenterPage()));
        },
        child: Container(
          margin: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 14),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: isEInk ? Colors.white : null,
            gradient: isEInk ? null : LinearGradient(
              colors: state.isFounder 
                  ? [const Color(0xFFFFF8E1), const Color(0xFFFFECB3)]
                  : [const Color(0xFFE0F7FA), const Color(0xFFB2EBF2)],
              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
              end: Alignment.bottomRight,
            ),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            border: Border.all(color: state.isFounder ? const Color(0xFFFFB300) : const Color(0xFF00ACC1), width: 1.8),
            boxShadow: [
              BoxShadow(
                color: (state.isFounder ? Colors.amber : Colors.cyan).withValues(alpha: 0.25),
                blurRadius: 8,
                offset: const Offset(0, 3),
              )
            ],
          ),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: state.isFounder ? const Color(0xFFFF8F00) : const Color(0xFF0097A7),
                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                ),
                child: const Icon(Icons.workspace_premium_rounded, color: Colors.white, size: 22),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 14),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          state.isFounder ? "創始釣友" : "VIP 指揮官",
                          style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 15, color: state.isFounder ? const Color(0xFF4E342E) : const Color(0xFF004D40)),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 1),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: state.isFounder ? const Color(0xFFFF6F00) : const Color(0xFF00838F),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                          ),
                          child: Text(state.isFounder ? "FOUNDER" : "ACTIVE", style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 3),
                    Text(
                      state.isFounder ? "點擊檢視專屬身分卡與特權" : "氣象署專線運作中 • 點擊進入中心",
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: state.isFounder ? const Color(0xFF6D4C41) : const Color(0xFF00695C), fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, size: 18, color: Colors.blueGrey),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    return InkWell(
      onTap: () => Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const PremiumPage())),
      child: Container(
        margin: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: cardBg,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          border: Border.all(color: cardBorder, width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 1.0),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.workspace_premium_rounded, color: AppColors.bioGold),
            const SizedBox(width: 12),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text("升級老船長 Pro 旗艦版", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14, color: textPrimary)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 2),
                  Text("週費 / 年費主力 / 終身買斷方案", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: textSecondary)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded, size: 18, color: textSecondary),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSearchField(
    Color searchBg, 
    Color cardBorder, 
    Color textPrimary, 
    Color textSecondary,
    bool isClassic,
    bool isEInk,
  ) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 4),
      child: TextField(
        onChanged: (value) => setState(() => _searchQuery = value),
        style: TextStyle(color: textPrimary, fontSize: 13.5),
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          hintText: "搜尋測站或地名 (如: 石門 / 龍洞)...",
          hintStyle: TextStyle(color: textSecondary, fontSize: 12),
          prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search_rounded, size: 18, color: textSecondary),
          filled: true,
          fillColor: searchBg,
          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
          border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), borderSide: BorderSide(color: cardBorder, width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 0.5)),
          enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), borderSide: BorderSide(color: cardBorder, width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 0.5)),
          focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), borderSide: BorderSide(color: isEInk ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFF0077B6), width: 1.5)),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFilterChips(
    Color cardBg, 
    Color cardBorder, 
    Color textPrimary, 
    Color textSecondary,
    bool isClassic,
    bool isEInk,
  ) {
    final filters = ["全部", "👑 VIP專屬", "🌊 資料浮標", "⏱️ 潮位站", "🆓 免費體驗"];
    return Container(
      height: 38,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
      child: ListView.separated(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        itemCount: filters.length,
        separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 6),
        itemBuilder: (context, idx) {
          final f = filters[idx];
          final isSelected = _selectedFilter == f;
          return ChoiceChip(
            label: Text(
              f, 
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 11, 
                fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal, 
                color: isSelected ? Colors.white : textPrimary,
              ),
            ),
            selected: isSelected,
            selectedColor: isEInk ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFF0077B6),
            backgroundColor: cardBg,
            side: BorderSide(color: isSelected ? (isEInk ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFF0077B6)) : cardBorder, width: isEInk ? 1.5 : 0.5),
            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16)),
            onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _selectedFilter = f),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStationTile(
    StationModel station, 
    bool isFavorited, 
    bool isPro,
    Color textPrimary,
    Color textSecondary,
    bool isClassic,
    bool isEInk,
  ) {
    final bool isSelected = station.id == widget.currentId;
    final bool isLocked = station.isProOnly && !isPro;

    return ListTile(
      onTap: () {
        if (isLocked) {
          Navigator.pop(context);
          Navigator.push(context, MaterialPageRoute(builder: (_) => const PremiumPage()));
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            SnackBar(
              content: Text("🔒 [${station.name}] 為老船長 PRO 專屬外礁/浮標水文模型，請解鎖啟用！"),
              backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF0077B6),
              duration: const Duration(seconds: 3),
              behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
            ),
          );
        } else {
          ref.read(currentStationIdProvider.notifier).state = station.id;
          ref.read(selectedDateProvider.notifier).state = DateTime.now();
          Navigator.pop(context);
        }
      },
      dense: true,
      leading: CircleAvatar(
        radius: 12,
        backgroundColor: station.isBuoy ? Colors.indigo.withValues(alpha: 0.15) : const Color(0xFF0077B6).withValues(alpha: 0.15),
        child: Icon(
          station.isBuoy ? Icons.sensors_rounded : Icons.water_drop_rounded,
          size: 13,
          color: station.isBuoy ? Colors.indigoAccent : const Color(0xFF0077B6),
        ),
      ),
      title: Row(
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              station.name,
              maxLines: 1,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontSize: 13,
                fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
                color: isSelected ? (isEInk ? Colors.black : const Color(0xFF0077B6)) : textPrimary,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),

          if (station.isProOnly)
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5, vertical: 1),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.bioGold.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                border: Border.all(color: isEInk ? Colors.black : AppColors.bioGold.withValues(alpha: 0.4), width: 0.8),
              ),
              child: Text(
                "👑 PRO", 
                style: TextStyle(color: isEInk ? Colors.white : AppColors.bioGold, fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
              ),
            )
          else
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5, vertical: 1),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: const Color(0xFF10B981).withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
              ),
              child: const Text("免費", style: TextStyle(color: Color(0xFF10B981), fontSize: 9, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            ),
        ],
      ),
      subtitle: Text(
        "${station.stationType} • ${station.agency} (${station.id})",
        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: textSecondary),
      ),
      trailing: IconButton(
        icon: Icon(
          isFavorited ? Icons.star_rounded : Icons.star_border_rounded,
          size: 18,
          color: isFavorited ? AppColors.bioGold : textSecondary.withValues(alpha: 0.4),
        ),
        onPressed: () async {
          await ref.read(tideRepositoryProvider).toggleFavorite(station.id);
          ref.invalidate(favoriteStationsProvider);
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  /// 🌟 100% 真實海難救援與海象語音支援彈窗 (消滅任何造假店家)

            ),
          ],
        ),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,

              ),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  const Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text("海巡署 118 海上報案", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 13.5, color: AppColors.hazardCoral)),
                      SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text("遇險困礁、人員落海免費直撥", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: AppColors.hazardCoral)),

                      foregroundColor: Colors.white,

                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),

            // 2. 氣象署 166 語音
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: const Color(0xFF0077B6).withValues(alpha: 0.08),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF0077B6).withValues(alpha: 0.3)),
              ),
              child: Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text("中央氣象署 166 語音", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontSize: 13.5, color: textPrimary)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text("海象、風浪與天氣電話預報", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: textSecondary)),
                    ],
                  ),
                  OutlinedButton(
                    style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                      foregroundColor: const Color(0xFF0077B6),
                      side: const BorderSide(color: Color(0xFF0077B6)),
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                    ),
                    onPressed: () => _callNumber("166"),
                    child: const Text("撥打 166", style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 11)),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Text(

              style: TextStyle(color: textSecondary, fontSize: 11, height: 1.4),
            ),
          ],
